# Work Checkpoint

## 2026-10-28 — Native todo prelude on by default (completed locally; unreleased)

- **Request:** Make `/todo-eager on` the default for simpler UX. Baseline `4c95d4e`; intentional contract change.
- **Implementation:** `src/workspace/progress.rs` adds `eager_off` (serde default, skipped when false) and `State::eager()`; legacy stored `eager: false` now means default-on. `src/providers/direct.rs` `todo_prelude` applies it when progress is absent or not opted out. `src/app/progress.rs` `/todo-eager on|off` toggles both flags; the card always shows the prelude state.
- **Compatibility:** Default progress bytes are unchanged; only an explicit opt-out writes `eager_off`, which public0.1.34 strict readers refuse.
- **Verification:** fmt; strict Clippy clean; full suite 1,850 passed/two ignored (new `todo_prelude_defaults_on_and_only_an_explicit_off_is_persisted`, native body prelude assertion, `/todo-eager off` card test). No live provider call.
- **Next action:** None; commit.

## 2026-10-28 — Automatic todo-driven evolution (completed locally; unreleased)

- **Request:** Make todo automatic and use it as the basis for Eevee's evolution decision, with the best UX. Baseline `8ca7879`. Intentional contract change; DESIGN_RULES updated.
- **Implementation:** `src/workspace/progress.rs` `focus_step`/`focus_label`/`focus_position` (active, else first pending; replaces `active_label`), summary `next:`. `src/app/progress.rs` auto-adopts provider proposals when `lead.auto_form`, adopts user edits immediately, mode hint on the card. `src/app/evolution.rs` `todo_focus`/`follows_todo`/`todo_guide`; ambiguous follow-ups take the step's selection, unclassified requests outside planning conversations keep Implement with the step's form; effort uses the focus step. `src/ui.rs` companion/compact companion show `→ <form> · Todo n/m label` or `Todo proposal waiting · /todo`.
- **Not changed:** native `/todo-eager` prelude remains off by default (tri-state would need a persisted-schema change). 60×18 has no companion pane; `/todo` and `/evolution-preview` show the same facts. Form never changes mid-run; todo never sends or grants.
- **Verification:** fmt; strict Clippy clean; full suite 1,849 passed/two ignored, including `automatic_todo_follows_provider_progress_and_steers_the_next_send`, `focus_follows_active_then_next_pending_adopted_step`, `home_companion_names_the_todo_step_that_steers_evolution`. No live provider call or interactive terminal run.
- **Next action:** None; commit.

## 2026-10-28 — Prompt-intent/todo audit (completed locally; unreleased)

- **Request:** Verify that prompt-intent classification and todo extraction work. Baseline `77776c3`.
- **Findings:** Todos are never extracted locally; they come from provider observations (native `todo` tool, Codex `turn/plan/updated`, ACP `plan`, SDK `TodoWrite`) normalized by `src/workspace/progress.rs`, and require explicit `/todo-adopt`. The wiring and tests are intact. The native route only asks the model to build a list when `/todo-eager on` is set (off by default per contract). The local classifier (`src/app/evolution.rs`) misread `…뽑는 기능 잘 구현됐나 확인` as Implementation and `Investigate the crash and fix it` as read-only Diagnosis.
- **Fixed:** `verb_description`, `CHECKS`/`CHECKED` verification fallback and `REPAIRS` + diagnosis context in `classify`; regression `descriptive_verbs_checks_and_diagnosed_repairs_choose_the_requested_capability`.
- **Verification:** fmt; strict Clippy clean; full suite 1,846 passed/two ignored. No live provider call.
- **Next action:** None; commit.

Parked-draft review now uses `[Discard]`/`[Replace]` instead of a trailing `[selected]`.
- **Verification:** Rust `cargo fmt --all`; `cargo clippy --locked --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings` clean; `cargo test --locked --all-targets --all-features -- --test-threads=4` **1,845 passed/two ignored**. ## 2026-10-28 — PowerShell handoff blockers and connection retention (completed locally; unreleased)

- **Request / baseline:** Investigated the user's recurring `Handoff task changed...` plus `Invalid frozen request JSON: EOF...` in Windows PowerShell and apparent connection loss on later visits. Read checkpoint/task/active plan/design; started clean main at `3655f69`. No remaining wave, live/paid call, publication or strategy work was started.
- **Reproduced causes:** Provider switching after existing work implicitly retained a task-revision-bound synthetic handoff, even on cancelled switches. Ordinary Request saving advanced the task revision and strict handoff admission refused it (`artifact://10`). Receipt staging then parsed the absent payload and appended the reported EOF (`artifact://7`, also reproduced stale staged-receipt retention). Account Advanced separately discarded safe expiry/source errors (`artifact://11`). These are platform-neutral paths, not evidence of PowerShell JSON corruption.
- **Implementation:** `src/app/planning.rs` stopped automatic handoff selection on provider switches; `src/app/planning/receipts.rs` cleared earlier staged receipts and skipped blocked preparations. Explicitly accepted imports remained byte-identical and stale/foreign admission stayed strict. `src/providers/direct/config/owned.rs` preserved safe load/grant/JWT-expiry diagnostics; `src/app/agents.rs` displayed them in Advanced. Session-only remained default, explicit private plaintext save remained opt-in, and refresh tokens were still discarded. No hidden renew/retry/send or credential-store mutation was added.
- **Regressions / source proof:** Consumer regressions in `src/app/tests.rs`, `src/app/agents.rs`, `src/app/planning/receipts_tests.rs`; removed the obsolete synthetic-document assertion from the existing session-isolation test in `src/app/planning/tests.rs`. Rust1.90.0 `cargo fmt --all`, strict locked all-target/all-feature Clippy, `cargo test --locked --all-targets --all-features -- --test-threads=4` **1,841 passed/two ignored**, and locked debug build passed (`artifact://15`). All five new regressions executed and passed after the observed RED failures.
- **Actual runtime proof:** `target/debug/ev` printed0.1.34, SHA256 `56283d6dc6e0b875bde492e06f3a137d8fc4ccdc722079020f6b09f6b8130b4a`. Four actual120×36/60×18 Implement journeys crossed OpenAI/API→Compatible with cancelled and accepted switches, revision-changing follow-ups, session/private restarts and synthetic saved account expiry. All passed;18 loopback POSTs with exact canonical text/history/model/credential scope, no switch/setup/startup replay, explicit saved-source restoration, session-only credential absence, visible expiry, unchanged saved bytes and no network on expiry inspection. Screens/full redacted wire/results: `target/connection-fix-runtime-final`.
- **Environment / diagnostic attempts:** Rust LSP initially exited0 because rust-analyzer was absent; installed the component, reloaded it and obtained real receipt-stage/handoff references. Isolated terminal dependencies live only in `/tmp/eevee-connection-fix-venv` (pyte0.8.2/wcwidth0.2.13). Initial runtime attempts were not passes: a broad inherited context-leak assertion rejected the legitimate static runner-schema wording “Eevee's owned runner.” The temporary driver excluded static tool descriptions only from that context-name check, retained full original wire and unchanged canonical role/text/auth predicates, then passed all complete journeys. Original diagnostic evidence remains in `target/connection-fix-runtime-diagnostic`.
- **Documentation / cleanup:** Updated README connection storage/expiry and `/handoff-clear` workaround, binding lifecycle/receipt rules, changelog and troubleshooting. Removed the owned temporary smoke script after proof; no provider account/user data was accessed or modified.
- **Active task / exact next action:** No local repair remains; commit this verified session with its checkpoint. Public0.1.34 packages and any installed Windows executable remain unchanged: no push/tag/release was authorized. Native Windows PowerShell/macOS and actual provider authentication/expiry were not exercised; a future distribution request needs native qualification before publication. No current local blocker.

## 2026-10-26 — Task-scoped auto-approve and large file limits (completed)

- **Request:** Add an "approve for this task" option and remove inconvenient file size/count limits, with good UX.
- **Auto-approve:** `/auto-approve`, `/auto-approve-off`, Shift+A in an engaged (not freshly auto-presented) file or command review. State is `PlanningState::auto_approve` (in memory, reset with the planning state on task change/restart). `App::drive_auto_approval` (start of `reconcile_permission_review`, from any surface) answers the lead's Permission (option index 1 = allow once) or allow-once Approval via `auto_accept`, and reserves agent command callbacks via `execution::State::auto_approve`; questions, other partners and cancelling/quitting work are excluded. Header status shows `AUTO-APPROVE`/`자동 허용 중` (always visible) and the chip becomes `[code · auto-approve]`; each auto-approved item sets a notice. Footer hints mention `A`.
- **Limits:** `MAX_FILES` 1024, `MAX_BYTES` 4 MiB, review bound 2×MAX+1 MiB/1M lines, native `TOOL_CALL_LIMIT` 1024, per-round calls 64, `BODY_LIMIT` 8 MiB, frames 16 MiB, streams 512 MiB, source-read cache = MAX_FILES. Tests keep the old 1 MiB body/128 calls/16 per round via `#[cfg(test)]` so boundary fixtures stay small. Replacement patches show only the changed region (common prefix/suffix trimmed, 3 context lines). Model/user-facing limit texts updated. SDK sidecar limits unchanged (bridge10).
- **Performance fix:** see TROUBLESHOOTING 2026-10-26 (stamp-based revalidation, candidate-pair alias checks).
- **Changed files:** `src/providers/{writes.rs,writes/tools.rs,writes/observations.rs,writes/posix.rs,writes/tests.rs,direct.rs,direct/tools.rs,direct/tools/tests.rs,direct/coding/tests.rs,direct/config.rs,direct/config/owned.rs,codex.rs,codex/tests.rs,acp.rs,acp/files.rs,acp/files/tests.rs,agent.rs,file_preview.rs}`, `src/app/{cooperation.rs,commands.rs,motion.rs,keys.rs,tasks.rs,execution.rs,execution/tests.rs,planning.rs,planning/review.rs}`, `src/command_help.rs`, `src/ui.rs`, `src/ui/tests.rs`, `locales/{en,ko}/{auto_approve.ftl,providers.ftl}`, README, CHANGELOG, DESIGN_RULES, AGENT_COMPATIBILITY, TROUBLESHOOTING, this checkpoint.
- **Verification:** `cargo clippy --all-targets` clean; `cargo test` 1,681 passed/2 ignored (new: auto-approve file/command/Shift+A/header tests, changed-region review assertion). Release PTY probe (scratchpad `probe/auto_approve.py`): EN 120×36 and KO 60×18, `/auto-approve` then the real rustc create→edit→failing build→fix→build→run chain completed with zero reviews, 3 real runs, header showed the state, `/auto-approve-off` removed it. `agent_coding_smoke.py --profile native` (ordinary reviews) still passed. ## 2026-10-26 — Codex/Claude Code-like default capability (completed)

- **Request:** The flow must work like Codex/Claude Code: input → connected provider model acts; no "cannot create files in this project" problems.
- **Finding:** Capability came from keyword classification (`app/evolution.rs::classify`). A fresh session's operation defaults to Plan, and unclassified requests kept it, so common requests (`make a snake game in python`, `add a login page`, `implement dark mode`, `다크모드 구현해줘`, `README에 설치 방법 써줘`, `의존성 업데이트해줘`, `고쳐줘` after a diagnosis) were dispatched read-only: no write/command tools, so the model reported it could not create files.
- **Change:** `request_evolution` falls back to Implement when nothing classifies, unless `PlanningState::plan_conversation()` (Plan with a submitted request, thread or native Plan history) is true. `implementation_followup` accepts elliptical act-on-it phrases (EN/KO). Explicit Plan/Review and read-only wording unchanged; per-effect exact approval unchanged.
- **Changed files:** `src/app/evolution.rs`, `src/app/planning.rs`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/AGENT_COMPATIBILITY.md`, this checkpoint.
- **Verification:** `cargo clippy --all-targets` clean; `cargo test` 1,677 passed/2 ignored (new `ordinary_coding_requests_get_write_tools_like_other_agents`; one fixed-form expectation changed `Continue.` Plan→Implement on a fresh session). Ad hoc release-binary PTY journeys (scratchpad `probe/vague_request.py`): Chat Completions `make a snake game in python`, Anthropic `다크모드 구현해줘`, Gemini `add a login page` each advertised write tools, showed the auto file review and created `app/main.py` after Allow once. `agent_coding_smoke.py --profile native` passed both journeys. No live provider call.
- **Active task / next action:** None.

## 2026-10-26 — Agent command approval UX (completed)

- **Request:** Improve poor UX when using the agent.
- **Method:** Ran the real native coding journey (`scripts/agent_coding_smoke.py --profile native`, EN 120×36 / KO 60×18, real rustc, loopback Chat Completions peer) and inspected captures.

(3) Korean command reviews mixed in English "Directory/Executable/1 of 1/is editing".

New EN/KO messages for those lines. The smoke driver engages an auto-presented review before choosing.
- **Changed files:** `src/app/cooperation.rs`, `src/app/execution.rs`, `src/app/execution/tests.rs`, `src/app/suggestions/tests.rs`, `src/ui.rs`, `src/ui/execution.rs`, `locales/{en,ko}/providers.ftl`, `scripts/agent_coding_smoke.py`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/DESIGN_RULES.md`, this checkpoint.

Other opt-in smoke drivers that open command reviews were not rerun.

## 2026-10-26 — Smooth live file-generation preview (completed)

- **Request:** Files being generated must appear live in the output with a good experience; it felt too choppy.
- **Finding:** Native previews (`FilePreview` in `src/providers/direct/tools.rs`) published only on a decoded newline or every 256 wire bytes, so an unfinished line stayed frozen and then jumped. Anthropic Messages additionally buffers whole tool inputs by default, so its file content arrived in one burst. The UI loop already drains at 30 ms and coalesces same-item events.
- **Change:** Previews also publish when new bytes arrived and ≥33 ms passed since the last frame (no repeat without new bytes). Messages tool declarations carry `eager_input_streaming: true`. Credential-safe redaction still withholds the trailing unfinished word.
- **Changed files:** `src/providers/direct/tools.rs`, `src/providers/direct/tools/tests.rs`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/AGENT_COMPATIBILITY.md`, `docs/DESIGN_RULES.md`, this checkpoint.
- **Verification:** `cargo fmt`; `cargo clippy --all-targets` clean; `cargo test` 1,673 passed/2 ignored, including new `unfinished_file_lines_advance_each_frame_without_waiting_for_a_newline` and the eager-streaming advertisement assertion. No live provider or interactive terminal capture was run.
- **Active task / next action:** None. Optional: actual terminal capture against a slow local SSE fixture to tune the 33 ms interval.

## 2026-10-26 — Provider-independent native tool parsing (completed)

- **Request:** Make agent responses, file creation, code edits and builds work regardless of provider.
- **Finding:** All native dialects already advertise and execute the same file/command tools, so the cross-provider gap was strict wire parsing in `src/providers/direct/tools.rs`. Common OpenAI-compatible stream variants (no `index`, no `type`, `null`/empty optional fields, repeated names, missing IDs, `tool_calls:null`/`function_call:null`) failed the entire round, and continuations dropped `reasoning_content` (DeepSeek/Kimi thinking tool loops reject this) and per-call `extra_content` (Gemini OpenAI-compatible thought signatures). Native Gemini failed on part-less finishing candidates and argument-less calls.
- **Change:** Tolerant fragment assembly with ID/latest-call index inference and `call_<index>` fallback IDs; request-local echo of `reasoning_content`/`extra_content`; Gemini empty parts and `{}` args. Approval, writer, terminal-completion and refusal checks are unchanged. Wire requests (tool schemas, `strict`, Anthropic `max_tokens`) were not changed.
- **Changed files:** `src/providers/direct/tools.rs`, `src/providers/direct/tools/tests.rs`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/AGENT_COMPATIBILITY.md`, this checkpoint.
- **Verification:** `cargo fmt`; `cargo clippy --all-targets` clean; `cargo test` 1,672 passed/2 ignored, including the two new regressions `compatible_chat_variants_keep_calls_and_continuation_metadata` and `gemini_partless_finish_and_argumentless_calls_are_tolerated`. No live/paid provider call was made; this is fixture proof only. No strategy/backtest applies.
- **Active task / next action:** None. Possible follow-ups needing live evidence: OpenRouter `reasoning_details` echo, `strict` handling on non-OpenAI Chat Completions servers, configurable Anthropic-compatible `max_tokens`.

## 2026-10-16 — Ordinary conversation and provider architecture (active; supersedes EV10A)

- **Latest user scope:** Diagnose ordinary conversation not being understood and assess native-provider architecture without silently removing coding or authentication capabilities. EV10A was not implemented at this checkpoint and was deferred.
- **Verified diagnosis:** `providers::Input` has no conversation transcript; native protocol bodies contain one current user envelope. That envelope expressly asks for task planning, while the first Request also becomes the persistent task goal. Retained UI responses are not outbound history. Actual compatible-HTTP requests from the published v0.1.10 binary had message counts 1/1/1; the third omitted the second user fact and prior assistant output, and its current user content was planning JSON rather than plain text (`target/conversation-before/{requests,results}.json`). The fixture intentionally holds its third response; this was payload evidence, not a passing three-turn semantic or clean-exit claim.
- **Architecture constraints:** Ordinary native conversation needs application-owned role-preserving messages and separate system context. Existing external routes supply controlled edits, approvals/questions, provider-owned resume and distinct authentication. Removing them without replacement loses those capabilities; native Anthropic API credentials are not interchangeable with SDK/subscription credentials.
- **Decision boundary / exact next action:** Resolve whether the product should fully replace external integrations with an app-owned native agent/tool loop or retain them as explicit advanced integrations while native conversation becomes primary. Then implement/verify the selected complete path; do not silently downgrade tool/auth support. No provider correction has been claimed or committed yet. Rust LSP references were attempted but the server exited code 0.
- **Completed release:** v0.1.10 at `050da9a` is public. Native qualification `38868170636` and immutable publication `38869187969` passed. Public eight assets, seven checksums, five safe archives and Schema 11 warnings were verified; public Linux installation/lifecycle passed 23 checks including its nested 34 terminal scenarios; workbench passed 35 journeys; actual v0.1.9 Schema 10 → published Schema 11 migration/refusal/resume passed all three cases. Artifacts: `target/release-0110-public`, `target/release-0110-install-complete`, `target/release-0110-workbench`, `target/release-0110-schema-public`.
- **Release harness note:** The first outer installation command expired after 360s despite the suite containing an 1100s nested terminal allowance. The complete run used a sufficient outer allowance and passed; no test assertion/internal deadline was changed. ## 2026-10-16 — PT06A–PT06C party routing and v0.1.9 release (released; v0.1.9)

- **User scope:** Implement tasks through PT06 (PT06A, PT06B, PT06C) and release. Commit without a Claude co-author trailer.
- **PT06A (`008ce2c`):** `store::Attribution` (partner id, name snapshot, form) is stamped on `runner::Prepared` and `ProviderRecord` via optional serde fields, so older records keep exact bytes and read `partner unknown`. Store validation rejects invalid ids/forms/control-character names. Approval details, run history and the provider journal display it. No packet/handoff path serializes these records.
- **PT06B (`10d51a5`):** Planning request state is keyed by partner (`Slot { owner, task, generation }`), bound at preparation from the lead (`front`). Events route only when task + generation match the bound slot; a different partner's later request refuses earlier late events; an idle released owner's slot is dropped. Lead switching is allowed during work (`/lead`, `/party` stay reachable); the request/approval stay with the owner; the second partner's send is refused with the owner's name and its draft kept. A failed request returns only to the sender's draft. Command preparation is routed by (task, owner, request id).
- **PT06C (`906e0a0`):** `Bolt (Jolteon) wants to run: …` / `wants permission` / `asks · QUESTION` and `Stop …'s command` in run approval/cancel and provider permission/approval/question dialogs; row counts unchanged so 60×18 still shows choices.
- **Verification:** Rust 1.90.0 `cargo test --locked --all-targets` 624 passed / 2 ignored; strict Clippy and fmt clean; release build. Actual-TUI PT06 driver (throwaway, scratchpad) passed at 120×36 and 60×18: two partners each prepared/approved a real command, approvals named `Eevee #1 (Jolteon)` / `Eevee #2 (Vaporeon)` with Stay/Approve visible, stored run records carried the partner snapshots, and history showed them (`target/pt06-tui/results.json`). Workbench regression suite passed all 35 journeys (`target/pt06-workbench/results.json`) using `target/ux-venv` (pyte 0.8.2).
- **Publication:** Published v0.1.9 from `887c116`. Native qualification `38841536756` passed Check and all five build/test/install rows; publication `38842493846` succeeded. All eight public assets downloaded, all seven SHA256SUMS entries matched, and the public linux-x86_64 archive holds `ev`, `LICENSE`, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` and reports `ev 0.1.9`. The published binary passed the PT06 TUI journey at 120×36 and 60×18 (`target/release-019-public-pt06/results.json`).
- **Environment note:** `gh` is not installed on this (WSL) host; run/release status was read from the public GitHub REST API with `curl`. Throwaway TUI drivers stayed in the session scratchpad.

Twenty keys at 250ms intervals produced 2 observed DB-change intervals while typing, 3 including the final quiet save, versus the 20-interval baseline. Native ordinary editing produced zero full-screen clears, with balanced frame markers and restored console modes/primary buffer. Six native dark/light/mono-ASCII journeys passed navigation, popup return without residue, CJK/combining paste, shrink/grow, nine forms, idle silence and exit (`target/native-014-{persistence,ui}`).
- **Measurements:** `target/performance-after/results.json`: 30-sample warm-start p95 37.83ms, streamed-workload key-to-render p95 13.10ms, 60s idle one-core CPU 0.417%, sampled idle RSS 10,137,600 bytes; all local targets met. The 10 MiB workload retained its 1 MiB spool bound; the 10,000-line diff remained read-only. These are local WSL, images-off measurements, not model latency or fleet benchmarks.
- **Additional evidence:** The real published 0.1.3 binary migrated Schema 6 to 8 with task preservation, refused downgrade byte-for-byte, and current 0.1.4 resumed (`target/014-schema-upgrade`). Final actual Codex streaming runs showed ten animated spinner frames / zero static frames, 156 / 68 partial-response screen updates, follow pause/resume, final text, idle silence and restoration (`target/response-ux`).
- **Changed files:** `src/app{.rs,/autosave.rs,/keys.rs,/tasks.rs,/events.rs,/editing.rs,/memory.rs,/tests.rs}`, `src/main.rs`, `src/platform/terminal.rs`, both terminal/performance smoke scripts, Cargo metadata, README, design rules, changelog, troubleshooting and this checkpoint. Earlier UX work is committed as `e9cc7b1`.
- **Release:** Published v0.1.4 at exact candidate `88f03121a79ded09af0dd32e89fd99fd0f7fcd06` after successful native qualification `38703367315` and publication `38704171970`. All eight public assets and seven manifest checksums were verified (`target/release-014-download/verification.json`); the public Linux installation lifecycle and 34 installed terminal cases passed (`target/release-014-installed-linux-verified`). Native published-package lifecycle reached the installed terminal probe but its helper build hit an UNC incremental-lock limitation; that full lifecycle is not claimed complete.

Public Actions API plus SSH git push are available; `gh` and API credentials are absent. Claude/ACP authentication conformance and native macOS GUI evidence are not claimed.

## 2026-10-15 — PT04B add a partner

- **Delivered:** the roster's `a` key and contextual `/add` (a `Choice` that is disabled with the capacity reason at six members) call `App::request_add_partner`, which queues `store::PartyRequest::Add`; `party_request`/`party_inflight` keep a second add from doubling up while the write is in flight. `Command::Party` → `State::add_partner` inserts `(NULL nickname, form 0, met_at = real clock, met_derived = 0, created_project = current project)` inside an `Immediate` transaction that re-counts seats and refuses at `MAX_PARTNERS` with "at most 6 members" before any commit; `Event::Party` returns the committed roster (or the refusal) and the app swaps `party` wholesale. ## 2026-10-15 — Token-economy dedup + provider-hub logic fixes

- **Delivered:** Local, deterministic request deduplication across the basket and Codex baseline. `context::execute` Assemble now emits `{source_root, captures:[…]}`: the shared repository root is hoisted once, `source_sha256` serializes as hex, and a capture byte-covered by a wider capture of the same `(root, path, revision)` emits `same_bytes_as` instead of duplicated text — the citation target is always a maximal (text-bearing) entry, so chains are impossible. Codex `edit_baseline` cites a byte-identical whole-file capture rather than embedding the file twice. `Payload.deduplicated_bytes` and `Receipt.deduplicated` surface savings in the basket drawer and request receipt, labelled local-only.
- **Logic fixes:** An oversized ACP tool observation (`>32 KiB`) previously aborted the entire provider session; `Item::tool_snapshot` now bounds `rawOutput`/`content` to a labelled head/tail slice with `truncated_by_eevee`/`total_bytes`/`omitted_bytes`, falling back to the prior bounded error only when still over the journal bound. A session credential attached to a non-Devin provider is a preview blocker and spawn-time injection is gated on the Devin profile. Devin token exchange reads at most 64 KiB of response JSON. `Connected (session credential)` now checks the credential's JWT expiry at render time instead of a flag set once at login.
- **Bug caught during implementation:** First dedup pass could produce citation chains (a covered capture citing another covered capture, leaving the packet without the bytes). Fixed by restricting citation targets to maximal captures; recorded in `docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md`.
- **Verification:** `cargo fmt` clean; `cargo clippy` only the two pre-existing warnings; `cargo test` — 376 passed / 0 failed, including new tests for dedup ordering, revision separation, baseline citation, truncated tool snapshots and receipt disclosure. No provider request run; changes are assembly/serialization-level and verified by unit tests over exact packet JSON.
- **Docs:** `docs/DESIGN_RULES.md` gained the structural-dedup rule; `task.md` T03 records partial implementation; CHANGELOG merged into the `2026-10-15` heading; prior checkpoint entry reworded to drop the reference-repo mention.

## 2026-10-15 — Devin website OAuth + provider hub session credential

- **Delivered:** Eevee-owned authorization-code + PKCE login for Devin's Windsurf backend (`server.codeium.com`), built against the provider's Connect-protocol metadata. A loopback listener on localhost receives the redirect; remote/headless sessions accept a pasted redirect URL, `?code=…` query, or `code#state` fragment instead. The returned session token becomes a `SessionCredential` that exists only in process memory (no serde, redacted Debug, bytes zeroed on drop) and is injected into the `devin acp` child environment as `WINDSURF_API_KEY`, where it beats the CLI's stored credentials. Expiry comes from the JWT `exp` minus a five-minute skew and surfaces as a review blocker, not a silent failure. Native `devin auth login` success clears the in-memory credential.
- **Provider-side support:** `runner::native::Process::spawn_piped_env` applies per-child environment overrides that stay out of the reviewed spec; Windows builds a fresh sorted UTF-16 `CREATE_UNICODE_ENVIRONMENT` block instead of inheriting a possibly-stale parent environment. `Input::credential` carries the memory-only credential from the planning drawer into the ACP preview (disclosure line) and spawn (injection). `/connect` now mirrors `/provider`'s active sync; refresh no longer wipes in-session auth outcomes; a live session credential reports `Connected (session credential)`; an open picker cannot survive `invalidate_authentication`, and a catalogue refresh that drops the selected model says so explicitly.
- **Verification:** `cargo fmt`, `cargo clippy` (only two pre-existing warnings: `artwork.rs:866` explicit-counter-loop and `auth.rs:817` function-item cast), `cargo test` — 373 passed, 0 failed, 2 ignored. New unit tests cover redirect/query/fragment code parsing, token plausibility (length, shell bytes, whitespace), JWT `exp` extraction with skew and malformed-segment rejection, and credential Debug redaction/expiry reporting. No live OAuth or provider request was run; `devin_web::login`'s actual browser round-trip is exercised only by an interactive Connect action.
- **Docs:** `docs/DESIGN_RULES.md` records the memory-only credential contract and injection precedence; README's provider table and connect note describe the website OAuth route and its exception to the "never collects tokens" rule. CHANGELOG has a `## 2026-10-15` entry.

## Previous checkpoint — Phase 1 native terminal shell

- Active task: implement the Eevee workbench in small, verified increments. Phase 1 is complete; the full agent workbench remains in progress.
- Exact next action: read this checkpoint, `AGENTS.md`, `task.md`, and Phase 2 of `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`. Implement durable task creation/resumption and goal storage through a SQLite worker as the next increment; verify create/edit/exit/reopen in a temporary data directory before adding repository evidence.

- Current boundary: only the cwd and session-local state are real inputs. No repository inspection, provider execution, permissions, persistent tasks, or durable memory exists yet. Goals are discarded on exit. Form changes never invoke a model. The blocking terminal event loop has no idle timer; worker events and async coordination are deferred until there is background work.
